# Graph Report - markdown-viewer-app  (2026-10-05)

## Corpus Check
- 3240 files · ~4,901,772 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 332 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .xsd 117, .tmpl 71, .ttf 54)

## Summary
- 48868 nodes · 70211 edges · 3152 communities (2801 shown, 351 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 98% EXTRACTED · 2% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 1321 edges (avg confidence: 0.91)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `f954a6ea`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
